### [2026-09-24T19:55:50.820459+00:00] tool · tool: terminal

{"output": "=== todiadiyatmo Readme.md (capital R) ===\n# Qwen3.8-Flash-Next on 4× RTX 3090\n\nA recipe for running **Qwen3.8-Flash-Next** (125B MoE, hybrid GDN + sparse attention, PLE n-gram table) on\nAmpere-class GPUs such as the **NVIDIA RTX 3090**. The goal is a large usable KV cache on four 24 GiB cards,\npractical single-request throughput, and the PLE lookup table kept in host RAM instead of VRAM.\n\nEverything here is version-pinned: a specific vLLM nightly, a specific set of patches, a specific chat template.\nIt is not a claim that stock vLLM or every Ampere GPU can run this model.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **4× RTX 3090 24 GiB**, or an equivalent Ampere-or-newer setup with 24 GiB per GPU. The reference rig runs a 200 W\n  power cap per card, PCIe only (no NVLink), some cards on Gen1 x4 risers. Topology and power limits change the numbers below.\n- **Host RAM for the PLE table plus runtime overhead.** The PLE lookup table is ~95 GiB in BF16 (Intel checkpoint) and\n  ~48 GiB in FP8 (W4A16-Attn8-FP8PLE checkpoint). Measured host RAM in use: **~85 GiB** with FP8 PLE, **~149 GiB** with\n  BF16 PLE (both measured 20 Sep 2026 while serving; the BF16 figure supersedes an earlier \"~120 GiB\" estimate).\n  Plan for at least **96 GiB** (FP8 PLE) or **160 GiB-class** (BF16 PLE); the reference host has 157 GiB usable and\n  the BF16 table leaves only ~8 GiB of it free.\n  Avoid swapping.\n- **NVMe storage**: the checkpoint is ~124 GB (W4A16-Attn8-FP8PLE, revision of 13 Sep 2026 or later) or larger (Intel), plus Docker layers and compile caches.\n  Budget a few hundred GB.\n- **Docker Engine, Docker Compose v2 and the NVIDIA Container Toolkit**, with all four GPUs visible to containers.\n  The whole recipe is a stock vLLM Docker image plus an overlay of patched source files; no bare-metal Python install.\n- **An NVIDIA driver compatible with the CUDA 13.0 base image** (reference host: 610.57.04).\n- **Optional: GPU P2P** via the patched driver from\n  [aikitoria/open-gpu-kernel-modules](https://github.com/aikitoria/open-gpu-kernel-modules) (branch matching your driver,\n  e.g. `610.57.04-p2p-v3`; needs Above 4G Decoding, Resizable BAR and `iommu=pt`). Not installed by this recipe; see\n  [P2P notes](#p2p-notes) below.\n- A complete checkpoint (weights, index, config, tokenizer, processor files) and the **Sharp v22.4.0 chat template**\n  (download step below). Accept the upstream license terms before downloading.\n\n## Recipe\n\n### Recipe 1 — TP2 × PP2, with or without MTP\n\nTwo Compose files, same settings except one line:\n\nPick the file that matches your checkpoint:\n\n| File | Checkpoint | Speculative decoding | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| [`recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2-mtp-attn8.yml`](recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2-mtp-attn8.yml) | Attn8-FP8PLE | MTP, 2 draft tokens | **The reference configuration** — what the rig serves. Vision on, `GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION` 0.94. |\n| [`recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2-intel-autoround.yml`](recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2-intel-autoround.yml) | Intel AutoRound | off (not available) | BF16 PLE table: **~149 GiB host RAM**, and needs the PLE gate patch. `GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION` 0.88 — its weights are ~0.6 GiB/rank larger. |\n\nThe two generic files below take any checkpoint via `MODEL_DIR` and are what the named files above are built from:\n\n| File | Speculative decoding | Use when |\n|---|---|---|\n| [`recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2-mtp.yml`](recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2-mtp.yml) | MTP, 2 draft tokens | You want faster generation. Decode is about 1.6× (prose) to 1.9× (code) faster; the KV pool is about 45% smaller. |\n| [`recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2.yml`](recipe/yml-tp2-pp-2.yml) | off | You want the largest KV pool (about 995,000 tokens). |\n\nIntel cannot use the MTP files: it ships `model_extra_tensors.safetensors` (a BF16 draft head), not the\n`mtp-model-*.safetensors` this vLLM loads.\n\nvLLM is pinned to nightly **`eed1f3d0c6043bd494424a22443ee198dd56f657`** (12 Sep 2026, image\n`vllm/vllm-openai:nightly-eed1f3d0c604…`, digest `sha256:d0742e7e…`) plus the patches in the next section, built locally as\n`local/qwen38-flash-next:eed1f3d0-ampere-pp-mtp`. The image reports its own version as `0.1.1.dev50+geed1f3d0c`; the commit\nhash is the identity. MTP needs the checkpoint revision that ships `mtp-model-*.safetensors` (13 Sep 2026 or later).\n\n**Why TP2 × PP2 instead of TP4?** The model has only **two KV heads**, so its KV cache can be split across\ntwo GPUs, not four. With **TP4**, the heads have to be duplicated across GPUs rather than split further.\n\n**TP2 × PP2** uses the same four GPUs as two pairs: each pair splits the two KV heads, and handles only half\nof the model's layers. This halves the attention KV memory needed per token on each GPU, giving roughly\n**2× the KV cache capacity compared with TP4**—more room for cached conversations.\n\nThe trade-off: pipeline stages sometimes wait for each other during prompt processing. Speculative decoding with MTP\nworks under pipeline parallelism since this vLLM base (upstream #46994 plus a local fix for this model's draft head).\nDFlash and DSpark are not available for this model in vLLM.\n\n## vLLM patches\n\nThe base image is stock; the overlay replaces 11 source files (full detail, upstream links and rebase notes in\n[`recipe/patch/README.md`](recipe/patch/README.md)):\n\n| Change | Purpose |\n|---|---|\n| [#54846](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54846), manually ported | FP8 KV cache on the QSA (sparse attention) path. |\n| **Local: Ampere `KV_FP8_BYTES` byte path** | RTX 3090 has no FP8 tensor cores and Triton has no fp8 type on sm86, so the cache is stored as raw E4M3 bytes and decoded to BF16 in kernel registers. |\n| **Local: Ampere FP8 PLE lookup** | Upstream's pinned-host PLE lookup kernel refuses fp8 on sm86. The kernel only copies bytes, so the gather runs as `uint8`; the result is bit-identical. |\n| Local fix for [#54709](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/54709) | Placement-aware PLE checks so PP>1 is accepted. |\n| [#54793](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54793) | Handle empty KV groups under PP. |\n| [#54795](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54795) | Intersect compatible KV layouts across workers. |\n| **Local mirror of [#46994](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46994) for `qwen4_exp`** | Upstream enables MTP under PP but fixes the draft head only for `qwen3_5_mtp`. Without this 3-line fix the `qwen4_exp` drafter skips its `fc` projections on the last stage. |\n| **Local: PLE gate for unquantized tables** | `from_quant_config` has no branch for \"body quantized by INC, PLE table not quantized\", so an INC/auto-round checkpoint with a BF16 PLE table (e.g. the Intel checkpoint) dies at boot with `NotImplementedError ... INCConfig`. Adds one branch that trusts the checkpoint's own INC rule (`.*ple.*` = 16-bit float); anything not provably unquantized still raises. Upstream-bound. |\n| [#55506](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55506), **still open upstream** | Index the mamba spec-decode block tables by request slot, not batch row. Without it, PP + MTP + prefix caching poisons recurrent state and a share of requests loop on one token forever. Added after the v1.1.0 validation and measured on its own — see [Known failure modes](#known-failure-modes). |\n\nAlready in the base (patches in the previous recipe, not needed any more): #53899 host-resident PLE table, FP8 PLE table\nloading with `--quantization inc`, #55375 fused-PLE stride fix, #55745 draft-broadcast stream guard, #46994 MTP under PP.\n\n`recipe/patch/SHA256SUMS` records the overlay hashes; `nightly-eed1f3d0-flashnext-mtp.patch` (9 files),\n`pr55506-mamba-spec-block-table-req-slot.patch` (2 files) and `ple-inc-bf16-gate.patch` (1 file, applies on top of the\nfirst) are the same changes as unified diffs for audit/rebase (the\nDockerfile copies the overlay and does **not** apply the diffs; do not do both).\n\n```bash\ndocker build -t local/qwen38-flash-next:eed1f3d0-ampere-pp-mtp recipe/patch\n( cd recipe/patch && sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS )\n```\n\n### Measured reference results\n\nAll throughput values are **tokens per second**, one request at a time (C=1), measured with club-3090 `bench.sh`\n(3 warm-ups, 5 measured runs). Prefill is **cold** (cache-busted) at ~10K and ~100K input tokens. Decode excludes time to\nfirst token. Checkpoint: W4A16-Attn8-FP8PLE, vision on, `--max-num-seqs 3`, utilization 0.94.\n\n| Recipe (vLLM `eed1f3d0`) | KV pool (tokens) | Cold prefill 10K / 100K | Decode prose / code | Draft acceptance | Free VRAM under load, tightest GPU |\n|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n| TP2 × PP2, **MTP n=2** (`yml-tp2-pp-2-mtp.yml`) | 526,909 | 4,404 / 4,601 | **131.8 / 160.5** | 70% (position 1: 84%, position 2: 68%) | 904 MiB |\n| TP2 × PP2, no speculation (`yml-tp2-pp-2.yml`) | **1,017,569** | 4,380 / 4,583 | 83.8 / 84.1 | — | 1,144 MiB |\n| TP2 × PP2, MTP n=3 (not shipped) | 521,742 | 4,325 / 4,520 | 131.9 / 171.5 | 53% | 860 MiB |\n\nThe MTP n=2 row is the 13 Sep 2026 run of this repository as published: image built from `recipe/patch/Dockerfile`, booted\nwith `yml-tp2-pp-2-mtp.yml` from a clean compile cache (boot to healthy 422 s). Its KV pool and the no-speculation pool\n(1,017,569, boot 302 s) come from that run; the no-speculation throughput and the n=3 row come from the same image and\ncheckpoint one day earlier (KV pool 995,019 / 540,016 in that session; the ±2% boot-to-boot spread applies).\nMTP does not change prefill; it trades KV pool for decode speed. n=3 adds 6.6% on code and nothing on prose, so n=2 is\nthe shipped default.\n\nSame run, MTP n=2: quality suite **68/75** pass@1 (Task 83 protocol, three earlier boots without MTP: 66–69), streaming\ntool-call probe **60/60**, prefix cache hit on the second call of a 29K-token prompt (25,472 cached tokens), 0 OOM.\n\nPrevious recipe (`v1.0.0-e962733e`, 5–8 Sep 2026, same checkpoint, no speculation): KV pool 941,463, prefill 10K / 90K\n4,355 / 4,183, decode 72.3. The new base alone gives about +16% decode and +5.7% KV pool.\n\nNotes on reading this table:\n\n- KV pool is the engine's logical token capacity for the whole engine, not per GPU and not the context limit.\n  Boot-to-boot allocation varies by about ±2%.\n- Decode differences below ~11% are inside this rig's noise.\n- The MTP profiles leave less than 1 GiB free per GPU under load. Watch for OOM on other GPUs or with larger images.\n\n### vLLM config\n\n**GPU-to-GPU transfers (P2P).** The benchmarks in this README used a patched NVIDIA driver that lets GPUs\nexchange data directly, rather than through system RAM.\n\n- **With a P2P-capable driver:** set `NCCL_P2P_DISABLE=0` and `NCCL_P2P_LEVEL=SYS` in `.env`.\n  These settings let NCCL use P2P, but cannot add P2P support to a driver that lacks it.\n- **Without driver-level P2P support:** set `NCCL_P2P_DISABLE=1` in `.env`.\n  Transfers between GPUs will go through system RAM instead, so multi-GPU communication will be slower.\n\nOn this rig, enabling P2P reduced an 80 MB all-reduce (a data-sharing operation across GPUs) from **26 ms to 5 ms**.\nIt also made processing a 100K-token prompt (prefill) **1.9× faster**. Token generation (decode) changed very little,\nbecause its main bottleneck is reading model weights from GPU memory, not transferring data between GPUs.\n\n**Benchmark your own setup if you run without P2P**—do not assume the reference speeds here will carry over.\n\n- Hardware: RTX 3090 ×4, 24 GiB per GPU, 200 W power cap per card; ~157 GiB usable host RAM.\n- vLLM configuration (current W4A16-Attn8-FP8PLE profile; identical for Intel except the model path):", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "=== The KEY overlay file: model_state.py — how did they fix the PP ban? ===\n# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: Copyright contributors to the vLLM project\n\"\"\"Model-runner state for Qwen4Exp PLE inputs.\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\nfrom vllm.config import VllmConfig\nfrom vllm.v1.worker.gpu.input_batch import InputBatch\nfrom vllm.v1.worker.gpu.mm.encoder_cache import EncoderCache\nfrom vllm.v1.worker.gpu.model_states.mamba_hybrid import MambaHybridModelState\nfrom vllm.v1.worker.gpu.states import RequestState\n\n\nclass Qwen4ExpModelState(MambaHybridModelState):\n    \"\"\"Add rollback-safe PLE n-gram context to the model inputs.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        vllm_config: VllmConfig,\n        model: nn.Module,\n        encoder_cache: EncoderCache | None,\n        device: torch.device,\n    ) -> None:\n        super().__init__(vllm_config, model, encoder_cache, device)\n        config = self.model_config.hf_text_config\n        self.uses_ngram_embedding = bool(config.ple_layer_ids)\n        if not self.uses_ngram_embedding:\n            self.ngram_context_len = 0\n            self.ngram_eos_token_id = 0\n            return\n\n        # vllm#54709: PP gate moved to config.py (placement-based).\n\n        self.ngram_context_len = int(config.ngram_size) - 1\n        if self.ngram_context_len <= 0:\n            raise ValueError(\"N-gram embedding requires context length >= 1.\")\n        self.ngram_eos_token_id = int(config.eos_token_id)\n        # PLE runs inside captured regions, so these buffers keep a fixed shape\n        # and address as the active request count changes between replays.\n        self.ngram_context = torch.full(\n            (self.max_num_reqs, self.ngram_context_len),\n            self.ngram_eos_token_id,\n            dtype=torch.int32,\n            device=self.device,\n        )\n        self.ngram_context_offsets = torch.arange(\n            -self.ngram_context_len,\n            0,\n            dtype=torch.int64,\n            device=self.device,\n        )\n        self.ple_query_start_loc = torch.zeros(\n            self.max_num_reqs + 1,\n            dtype=torch.int32,\n            device=self.device,\n        )\n\n    def _prepare_ngram_context(\n        self,\n        input_batch: InputBatch,\n        req_states: RequestState,\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        num_reqs = input_batch.num_reqs\n        context = self.ngram_context\n        context.fill_(self.ngram_eos_token_id)\n        if num_reqs == 0:\n            return context\n\n        request_indices = input_batch.idx_mapping[:num_reqs].long()\n        context_end = req_states.num_computed_tokens.gpu[request_indices].long()\n        token_indices = context_end.unsqueeze(1) + self.ngram_context_offsets\n        valid_tokens = token_indices >= 0\n        token_indices.clamp_min_(0)\n        context_tokens = req_states.all_token_ids.gpu[\n            request_indices.unsqueeze(1), token_indices\n        ]\n        context[:num_reqs].copy_(\n            torch.where(\n                valid_tokens,\n                context_tokens,\n                context_tokens.new_full((), self.ngram_eos_token_id),\n            )\n        )\n        return context\n\n    def prepare_inputs(\n        self,", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "=== ahnguyen17's patches README — what are sites 15/17/18 (the PP ring sync)? ===\n# Sites 15 / 17 / 18 — the MTP ring deadlock fix (2026-08-30)\n\nPatch scripts of record live at `~/site15.py`, `~/site17.py`, `~/site18.py` (host).\nApply pattern: `docker run -d --entrypoint sleep <base_img> infinity` → `docker cp` script →\n`docker exec python3 /tmp/sN.py` → verify with grep + `ast.parse` →\n`docker commit --change 'ENTRYPOINT [\"vllm\"]' <ctr> qwen38-flash-next:<next>`.\n\n## Site-15 — pure-torch post_update on non-last PP ranks (pp3fix8)\nFile: `vllm/v1/worker/gpu/model_runner.py`\n- Module-level `_post_update_pure_torch(...)`: boolean-mask valid rows, last-sampled gather,\n  ragged token scatter via `repeat_interleave` + within-block arange, `num_computed += query_len - num_rejected`.\n  One host sync (`ns.sum().item()`) — acceptable per decode step.\n- `postprocess_sampled()`: `if self.is_last_pp_rank:` keeps triton `post_update(...)`, else calls the port.\n- MANDATORY gate: bit-exact equivalence test of both paths before deploying — build ONE input\n  dict, `clone()` per arm (consecutive `make()` calls advance the RNG and fake a mismatch —\n  this false-positived once). Test masked rows (-1 in idx_mapping), zero-sampled reqs, ragged appends.\n\n## Site-17 — ring on main stream (pp3fix9)\nFile: `vllm/v1/worker/gpu/pp_utils.py`\n- `receive()` / `broadcast()`: drop `with torch.cuda.stream(self.broadcast_stream)` +\n  `wait_stream` + `record_stream` dances; run the two `torch.distributed.broadcast` calls on the\n  main stream; record the PendingRecv event on main stream.\n- Rationale: sibling-communicator + side-stream interleaving vs inter-stage p2p is a deadlock\n  hazard class. NECESSARY but INSUFFICIENT — v25 hung identically; the real gate was site-18.\n\n## Site-18 — unconditional ring participation (pp3fix10) ← THE FIX\nFile: `vllm/v1/worker/gpu/pp_utils.py`\n- `receive()`: `compute_need_sampled_mask` returning None no longer skips — substitute\n  `np.zeros(num_reqs, bool)` and ALWAYS enqueue the 2 broadcasts. Mask stays a per-request\n  DATA filter at consume time (`get_prev_sampled_outputs` already filters).\n- `broadcast()`: remove the early-return on mask None; ALWAYS broadcast; pad/truncate\n  `sampled_token_ids` to `max_sample_len` with -1 (consumers skip -1) so numel matches the\n  receivers' `[num_reqs, max_sample_len]` buffer.\n- Principle: **collective participation must be unconditionally symmetric across ranks;\n  rank-local state (pipeline-lagged `num_computed_tokens_np`) must never gate whether a\n  collective is issued — only what its payload means.**\n\n## Verification chain that led here\n1. v20 (no-MTP) served → bug isolated to spec path.\n2. v21 (site-15): hang MOVED to the port's first sync → disease upstream of post_update.\n3. v22 (drop GDN env): silent triton fallback, identical hang → env archaeology dead end.\n4. v23 (--enforce-eager): identical hang → graphs/compile exonerated.\n5. v24 (600s watchdog + NCCL dump): **named BROADCAST SeqNum=1 NumelIn=4 on pp_broadcast,\n   ranks 0/1 enqueued-never-started, rank 2 absent** → comms deadlock, skip-path suspected.\n6. v25 (site-17 main-stream ring): identical → interleaving wasn't the gate.\n7. Static read of PPHandler: only skip path = mask-based early return on rank-local state\n   → site-18. v26 = decisive boot.\n\n## If v26 still hangs\n- Check the NCCL dump again: a DIFFERENT collective/SeqNum = progress; same = the skip has\n  another gate (audit `sample_tokens` last-rank path for other early returns).\n- Layer-partition bisect (4/32/12, `--max-model-len 16384` if PP1 cache-block OOMs at 32 layers)\n  distinguishes placement vs comms — but comms now proven, so weight accordingly.\n- The mask's rank-local inputs (`num_computed_tokens_np`) can be made scheduler-derived\n  (identical on all ranks) if pad-filtering proves insufficient.", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "=== Fetch the full todiadiyatmo patch README for the complete overlay description ===\n# vLLM patches — nightly `eed1f3d0`\n\nThis directory packages the overlay tested on four RTX 3090s on September 12–13,\n2026, plus one patch added after that validation (recipe `v1.2.0-eed1f3d0`). It is not a\npatch set for arbitrary vLLM releases.\n\n## Pinned base\n\n- Image: `vllm/vllm-openai:nightly-eed1f3d0c6043bd494424a22443ee198dd56f657`\n- Digest: `sha256:d0742e7e31b16c85c9e215960604452f5f172a522d35c94ca42b7030cb0afa36`\n- vLLM commit: `eed1f3d0c6043bd494424a22443ee198dd56f657` (12 Sep 2026). The image reports\n  `vllm.__version__` as `0.1.1.dev50+geed1f3d0c`; treat the commit hash as the identity.\n- PyTorch `2.13.0+cu130`, CUDA 13.0.\n\n## Included changes (11 files)\n\n| Change | Files | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|\n| [#54846](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54846), manually ported | `models/qwen4_exp/nvidia/{qsa.py,ops/qsa.py}`, `platforms/interface.py`, `v1/attention/backends/utils.py` | FP8 KV cache on the QSA sparse-attention path. |\n| Local Ampere `KV_FP8_BYTES` path | same files | RTX 3090 has no FP8 tensor cores and Triton has no fp8 type on sm86. The cache is stored as raw E4M3 bytes and decoded to BF16 in kernel registers. |\n| Local Ampere FP8 PLE lookup | `models/qwen4_exp/nvidia/ngram_embedding.py` | Upstream's pinned-host PLE lookup kernel fails on sm86 with `type fp8e4nv not supported`. The kernel only moves bytes, so the gather runs as `uint8`; the result is bit-identical. |\n| Local fix for [issue #54709](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/54709) | `model_executor/models/config.py`, `models/qwen4_exp/nvidia/model_state.py` | Placement-aware PLE checks so pipeline parallelism is accepted. Upstream still rejects `pipeline_parallel_size > 1` for this model. |\n| [#54793](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54793) | `v1/core/kv_cache_utils.py` | Handle empty KV groups under PP. |\n| [#54795](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54795) | `v1/attention/backends/utils.py` | Intersect compatible KV layouts across workers. |\n| Local mirror of [#46994](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46994) for `qwen4_exp` | `models/qwen4_exp/nvidia/mtp.py` | #46994 enables MTP under PP but fixes the draft head only in `qwen3_5_mtp.py`. The `qwen4_exp` draft head still branches on `is_first_rank`, which is false on the last stage where the drafter lives, so it skips the `fc` projections. Branch on `intermediate_tensors is None` instead. |\n| [#55506](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55506), **still open upstream** | `v1/worker/mamba_utils.py`, `v1/worker/gpu/model_runner.py` | Index the mamba spec-decode block tables by request slot, not by batch row. Without it, PP + MTP + prefix caching poisons recurrent state and a share of requests degenerate into a constant-token loop. See the section below. |\n\nAlready in the base, **not extra patches** (they were patches in `v1.0.0-e962733e`):\n[#53899](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53899) host-resident PLE (now `ngram_embedding.py` and\n`config/engram.py`), FP8 PLE table loading with `--quantization inc`,\n[#55375](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55375) fused-PLE stride fix,\n[#55745](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55745) `record_stream` guard in draft broadcast, and\n[#46994](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46994) MTP under pipeline parallelism.\n\n## One patch has a different evidence basis\n\nNine of the eleven files were validated **as one package** on 12–13 September 2026: a boot, a bench and a\nquality run of the whole stack. That is enough to say the package works; it is not enough to attribute any\nresult to an individual file in it.\n\n`#55506` is different. It was added on 16 September and measured **on its own**, with an A/B that the other\nnine never got, because it fixes a fault we hit in production rather than one we anticipated.\n\n**The fault.** With pipeline parallelism + MTP + prefix caching, a share of requests degenerate into a\nconstant-token loop (`ductductduct…`, prompt-independent) until they exhaust the token budget. The loop token\nis what the sampler deterministically emits for an **all-NaN logits row**: the request's recurrent state has\nbeen poisoned and never recovers. On sm_80/86 the misdirected access stays mapped, so there is **no crash, no\nCUDA error and nothing in the log** — on sm_121 the same bug is loud ([#54173](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/54173)).\nDo not read the absence of errors as absence of the fault.\n\n**Why it happens.** `MambaSpecDecodeGPUContext` captures the block tables' raw data pointers exactly once, but\nthe V2 model runner bound that capture to the per-step *gathered* tables (batch-ordered, re-gathered every\nstep) while the copy kernels resolved rows as `batch_idx if HAS_IDX_MAPPING else req_idx`. Under async\nscheduling with PP, a non-last rank runs its postprocess `pp_size` steps after its batch was gathered, so the\nmapping it holds is stale and the copy walks another request's freed or reallocated block ids. In the unified\ncache layout main KV, GDN conv/SSM and PLE conv state all alias the same page and are distinguished only by\nblock-id ownership, so those writes land inside a live request's state. The fix binds the context to the\nsource per-request-slot tables and indexes by `req_idx` — the contract the V1 path already relied on.\n\n**Measured here** (17 arm-hours, 204 responses across five boots, TP2×PP2 + MTP n=2 on four RTX 3090s,\nthree requests in flight, agentic-shaped prompts of 8–20K tokens):\n\n| | loop rate | KV pool | free VRAM | decode prose / code | prefill 10K / 100K |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| without the patch | **9/72 (12.5%)** | 540,016 | 2,544 / 848 MiB | 127.8 / 158.7 | 4,288 / 4,516 |\n| with the patch | **0/132 (0.0%)** | 540,016 | 2,538 / 844 MiB | 128.0 / 158.3 | 4,330 / 4,445 |\n\nFisher exact, two-sided: **p = 0.00006**. Every performance number is inside the anchor's own run-to-run\nspread, and the KV pool is identical — this patch costs nothing measurable.\n\nTwo conditions matter for reproducing the fault: **three or more concurrent requests** (the reporter's\nthreshold is exactly 2→3 slots, and ours reproduced at 3) and long generations. A single-stream smoke test\nwill not show it.\n\n⛔ **Do not \"fix\" this with `--no-async-scheduling`.** Upstream threads recommend that flag for MTP × hybrid\ncorruption, and it is measured at roughly no cost on tensor-parallel-only rigs. On this pipeline-parallel\nrecipe it does not serve **at all**: the second pipeline stage blocks in `irecv_tensor_dict`, gloo times out\nafter 30 minutes and the engine dies — 36 of 36 requests failed in our arm. Boot, warmup and CUDA-graph\ncapture all succeed first, so the failure only appears on the first real request.\n\n## Build and audit\n\nFrom the repository root:\n\n```bash\ndocker build -t local/qwen38-flash-next:eed1f3d0-ampere-pp-mtp recipe/patch\n```\n\n`overlay/` contains 11 Python source files copied byte-for-byte from the tested\ndeployment. `SHA256SUMS` records their hashes and the unified diff's hash:\n\n```bash\n( cd recipe/patch && sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS )\n```\n\nThe Dockerfile copies the overlay; it does **not** run the unified patches.\n`nightly-eed1f3d0-flashnext-mtp.patch` (9 files) and\n`pr55506-mamba-spec-block-table-req-slot.patch` (2 files) are the audit/rebase alternative. Both apply to the\nbase commit with `patch -p1` (0 fuzz; #55506 lands with one hunk at offset 32 lines). The two diffs touch\ndisjoint files, so order does not matter. Do not apply either on top of the overlay.", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

**All three repos verified — and the picture is now complete.** The todiadiyatmo recipe is the winner and it's a near-exact match for our case:

| Requirement | todiadiyatmo recipe | Status |
|---|---|---|
| PP>1 with PLE | **#54709 placement-based gate**: allows PP>1 if PLE layers live on pipeline rank 0 (ours: layer 1 → rank 0 ✓) | ✅ Solved |
| Ampere (sm86) FP8 PLE lookup | **Local patch**: pinned-host PLE gather runs as uint8 bytes on sm86 (bit-identical) | ✅ Solved |
| Correct artifact | `W4A16-Attn8-FP8PLE` — **exactly what I already downloaded** (rev `aeae1483`, has `mtp-model-*` shards, INC/GPTQ-packed experts — a different loader path than the AWQ one that broke in V2) | ✅ Match |
| Public runtime | Stock vLLM nightly `eed1f3d0` (12 Sep) + 11-file overlay, **publicly pullable image** | ✅ Available |
| FP8 KV on Ampere | Optional `KV_FP8_BYTES` path (bf16 KV default) | 🔄 Later stage |
| MTP under PP | #46994 mirror + #55506 | 🔄 Later stage (MTP off in Stage A) |

The ahnguyen17 fork is a locally-built image (`pp3fix26`, not on a registry) targeting 176B/AWQ with mismatched-GPU PP3 — valuable reference, wrong base. loktar00 is benchmark evidence for a different pinned base. Also key: measured reference = **83.8 tok/s decode C1 no-spec, KV pool ~1.0M tokens on 4×24 GB** — far above our 20 tok/s/agent target.

My V3-adapted math for the artifact I downloaded: **70.9 GB GPU-resident / 6 GPUs = 11.8 GB/GPU at TP2×PP3** → ~6.5 GB/GPU headroom for KV+state. GDN state is the C8 binder, not KV.
